# Fixed / refuted bugs awaiting user verification

Rows moved here from [bugs.md](../../bugs.md) once an agent set FIXED or REFUTED. IDs are permanent; cite as `bugs.md #NNN`. User verifies, sets CLOSED, and moves the row to [bugs_closed.md](bugs_closed.md) (resolution text may be trimmed there; this file keeps the full text).

| # | Status | Bug Description | Resolution |
|---|--------|------------------|------------|
| 461 | FIXED | Scout "Meet With Mayor" does nothing (no diplomacy dialog opens with the colony owner). | Talk machine wedge: an Esc on any FUN_5bfb_153e talk popup left `s_talk.active` set, so every later encounter (Meet With Mayor, adjacent-unit talk) returned 0 silently until restart. Cancelled DIPLO_TALK results now end the talk (`ai_diplo_apply_popup_result`), and new game / load call `ai_diplo_talk_reset`. Headless repro: Meet → Esc → Meet queued no DIPLO_TALK before, queues greeting + CHOICE after. |
| 462 | FIXED | Upon being damaged, my Merchantman didn't properly disappear; the dissolve animation appears to have been rendered under it. It was still visible until my turn. | Dissolve was drawing the post-outcome frame with the ship still on it: a damaged hull with no own Drydock/Shipyard colony was left standing on its tile until an end-of-turn router moved it, so the "before" and "after" frames were identical. DOS teleports on the spot (see #463); `units_ship_enter_repair` now does, so the hull pixelates away inside the same dissolve pair. |
| 463 | FIXED | Pretty sure that when ships are damaged and go to Europe by fallback, they don't appear in the inbound lane, but rather are instantly teleported there and put on a timeout (as normal for damaged ships). Check DOS. | Confirmed against DOS: `FUN_5fef_0352` unlinks the loser (`FUN_281f_0812`) and re-places it (`FUN_281f_0844`) in BOTH arms — the decompile drops those register args at raw 99643-99644, the asm at `5fef:0ceb-5fef:0cf9` loads the nearest own Drydock colony's x/y, or `loser_nation - 0x14` in both coordinates (the off-map Europe slot, `5fef:0bc0-5fef:0bcb`) when there is no repair port; `units_ship_enter_repair` now hands a portless human hull to the Europe Expected lane immediately with the DOS repair timer as the wait, instead of leaving it on its tile for `turn_route_damaged_ships` to launch on a voyage roll. |
| 464 | FIXED | When ships are defeated by coastal forts, they should have the dissolve animation happen and then disappear. Animation was missing and the sprite didn't disappear when I expected it to. | `units_fort_vs_ship` despawned/relocated the target with no dissolve notify at all; DOS resolves fort fire through the real combat resolver (`FUN_364b_03f6` raw 57095 -> `FUN_291f_0a14` = `FUN_5fef_1b0e`), which presents its outcome with the `FUN_281f_03ea` fizzle — the outcome is now wrapped in `units_dissolve_notify(0)`/`(1)`, and the sunk/damaged hull leaves the map inside that frame pair. |
| 465 | FIXED | Coastal forts should not fire on neutrals or allies. At present they seem to fire on just about anyone; even nations I'm at peace with. | Fort gate now DOS-literal FUN_364b_03f6 raw 57082 (fire iff PEACE bit 0x40 clear, or Privateer) — the same byte F8 Foreign Affairs shows, so Peace there = no fire; the old Linux WAR-bit gate fired on pairs whose treaty writer (13b0 SIGNTREATY, 153e WORTHY/GIVECASH) set PEACE without clearing WAR; those writers now clear WAR too. Also one salvo per tile at the stack head, skipped when that ship is the colony's own. |
| 466 | FIXED | Something is wrong with hammer production and lumber consumption. In a colony where hammer production capacity exceeded lumber production, lumber seemed to go down on one turn (as it should; extra was taken from stockpile), and then the next turn returned to maximum (it should have continued to decrease, since usage exceeded production). | Autumn hammers freeze removed (turn.c Phase A/L, colony_preview.c, colony_screen.c): the only evidence for it, real-DOS colony-prod-tests Spring→Autumn 1680 pair, has zero staffed carpenters in all 32 colonies, so unchanged hammers there proved nothing; hammers now bank and debit lumber 1:1 every tick, `unit_hammers_lumber_two_turns` locks a strict two-turn drain pre- and post-1600. |
| 467 | FIXED | Pretty sure John Paul Jones' frigate is supposed to appear on a sealane tile somewhere, not adjacent to one of your colonies. Not sure. | FIXED 2026-09-16: DOS FUN_4345_0342 case 0xe spawns the Frigate in the nation's off-map Europe slot (raw 73044ff: `FUN_281f_095c(0x11, n, n-0x18, n-0x18)`, order 0, goto = stored Europe landfall tile), so it starts docked in Europe and sails over like a purchase; the port now docks the human's Frigate in the Europe harbor (`effect_jones_frigate`), AI nations keep the coastal stand-in (no AI Europe model). Test: deep Jones asserts harbor +1, map +0. |
| 468 | FIXED | "New Netherlands and New Spain have signed a new treaty." Incorrect country names; they are both pre-independence, and even after independence, the names would be different. | FIXED 2026-09-16: DOS fills diplomacy %STRINGs via FUN_281f_09a4 → FUN_15b3_01e0 = NAMES.TXT @NATIONALITY adjective (Dutch/Spanish, independence remap inside); `ai_diplo_rival_name` returned the New World country name. Now returns the adjective (`reports_nation_adjective_display_name`); every diplomacy popup/status line changes accordingly (test strings updated). |
| 469 | FIXED | "This type of unit cannot attack" during go-to locks the user out of proceeding further. Those enter reasons now park the unit and clear the order regardless of the popup (`game_pacer_goto_step`). |
| 470 | FIXED | Rival AI transports (Merchantman etc.) dawdle beside coastal forts and get sunk. | FIXED 2026-09-17: the AI fort-avoidance (`ai_euro_tile_under_enemy_fort_fire`) was gated on the WAR bit while the battery fires whenever the PEACE bit is clear or the hull is a Privateer (#465); the gate now matches the battery, the flee step runs outside the `at_war` block and drops a goto that points back under fire. The 4d2e wander itself stays DOS-literal (fears batteries only with goods aboard), so an empty hull can still stray beside a fort for a turn. |
| 471 | FIXED | Bought horses from Indians while the wagon train already carried 100 horses; the new horses merged into the same hold, giving an illegal 135 stack. | FIXED 2026-09-17: the village-buy cargo delivery merged uncapped into the matching hold; `FUN_0000_8f68` is `FUN_15eb_30b8`, the capped loader, so it now tops up to 100 and spills into a free hold. |

| 484 | FIXED | Treasure Trains cost only one hold: six of them fit a single Galleon, and the "treasure needs a Galleon" rule was a type-name check copied from the manual. | FIXED 2026-09-17: DOS charges a passenger its `@UNIT` **size** column (`DS:0x5238`) — `FUN_4720_00e0` (raw 74628-74665) seeds each hull with `0x5237[type] - unit+0x3150` (cargo capacity less goods holds) and first-fits each non-ship unit with `size <= room; room -= size`. Treasure size is 6 and Galleon cargo is 6, so the manual's Galleon rule is emergent, not a name test. `units_ship_free_passenger_slots` now sums rider sizes, `units_board_stacked` and `units_ship_departure_pickup` charge them, and `units_find_boardable_ship`'s `require_galleon` flag became `need_space` (the boarder's own size). | 488 | FIXED | AI Europeans got the Discoverer/Explorer Veteran Soldier handicap; in DOS it is human-only. | FIXED 2026-09-17: `FUN_75c2_235c` raw 121644-121647 gates the type-1 Soldier's @JOB 0x15 on `(bVar1 && difficulty < 2) \|\| nation == 2`, where `bVar1 = (player control byte 0x543f + nation*0x34) == 0` = this nation is the human; `units_starter_skills` took a bare `difficulty <= 1`. Added an `is_human` argument through `units_spawn_euro_starter_fleet`; Spain keeps its Veteran Soldier as an AI, French Hardy Pioneer (@JOB 0x14, raw 121637) is nation-only and unchanged. |
| 489 | FIXED | DOS starts **all four** Euro nations (the human included) in the off-map Europe lane, not on the map: `FUN_75c2_235c` raw 121612-121646 creates the ship and both passengers at `FUN_281f_095c(type, nation, nation-0x1c, nation-0x1c)` = sentinel (228+n, 228+n) with voyage counter `+0x315a = 0`, so `FUN_48d3_03d0` lands them on the landfall tile on the first Atlantic tick. The port puts the human fleet straight on the map tile (AI NEW WORLD fleets already use 228+n). Player-visible turn-1 restructure — prepare and ask. | **Premise refuted — no turn-1 restructure; 228+n is the LAST westbound Atlantic lane, not "in Europe"**: `FUN_48d3_06ba` walks the chain `244+n → 240+n → 236+n` (the Europe port) and `232+n → 228+n → 224+n` (where `FUN_48d3_064e`/`048e` places the hull on the map) for the ACTIVE nation only (raw 77965-77980; lane entries `FUN_48d3_007a` raw 77626 = `n-0x0c` and `FUN_48d3_0346` raw 77755 = `n-0x18`), so the human nation's own pass lands his fleet before the player ever sees turn 1 — confirmed by both genuine DOS turn-0 oracles, `original_saves/mapgen/SEED100.SAV` and `original_saves/COLONY00.SAV`, which show the human fleet already ON the landfall tile (goto restamped to that same tile) with only the three AI nations still at 229/230/231 — exactly what `units_new_world_start` already produced, so the sim is unchanged. What the row did expose is a save-interop defect, now fixed in `col1_bridge.c`: the port wrote its Europe harbor at `228+n` and Bound at `232+n`, so a real DOS westbound stack one tick from landing (lategame `COLONY06.SAV`: Dutch hulls at 231 with counter 0, chained Artillery and goto (53,56), vs the French at 233 with counter 1) imported as a docked ship and re-exported one lane too far out. Lanes are now `236+n` harbor / `228+n` Bound (counter 0 kept verbatim = "arrives next tick") / `244+n` Expected, which also resolves the old "unresolved fifth family" at `240+n` in save_format_map.md. |
| 490 | FIXED | AMERICA / TRIBE.TXT new games put the three AI Euro fleets straight on their @SCENARIO landfall tiles instead of the Europe sentinel (`ai_spawn_euro_fleet`'s `use_tribe_txt` branch, `src/core/ai.c:472-490`). DOS's `FUN_75c2_235c` is path-independent — all four nations are created at `(228+n, 228+n)` with counter 0 whatever the map source — so an AMERICA start should look like a NEW WORLD one. The branch is a workaround: on a scenario map the @SCENARIO tile IS the ship's Atlantic start, so aiming the Europe-exit goto at it left the fleet ordered to sail to the tile it already stood on and it never put its colonists ashore; the fix is to resolve that goto the DOS way, not to move the placement. Found while refuting #489; `original_saves/` holds no genuine DOS AMERICA turn-0 save to diff against. | FIXED 2026-09-18: `ai_spawn_euro_fleet` no longer branches on `use_tribe_txt` — every new game, generated or AMERICA/TRIBE.TXT, now creates all three AI fleets on the westbound Europe sentinel `(228+n, 228+n)` with voyage counter 0 and `goto` = the nation's landfall tile (`@SCENARIO` raw 121035 / NAMES.TXT: AMER2 (34,20) (39,10) (47,61) (50,33)), exactly as DOS `FUN_75c2_235c` raw 121612-121646 does path-independently, so the ported `FUN_48d3_06ba`/`048e` chain lands them on their landfall ring at the first EOT tick; the "aim at real coast instead" workaround (`ai_goals_nearest_landing_water_w`) is deleted, the human fleet still lands before turn 1 via `units_new_world_start`, and `unit_ai` now asserts lane/counter/goto pre-tick and the landing post-tick on both paths. |

| 521 | FIXED | AI land adjacent-attack pair (`ai_euro_land_best_adjacent_foe`, `ai_euro_land_try_adjacent_attack`) has no DOS counterpart but is a golden-backed stand-in: the ported `LAB_521d_4d2e` attack term (raw 88880-88940) needs `owner >= 0 && at_war` from the destination tile's layer3 owner nibble, so a foe on an unclaimed tile is never scored; without the stand-in `golden_woi_ref01` REF cannot reduce defended colonies. Real fix: make the 4d2e attack term reachable on land and/or bind every eligible unit to a 0a60 MILITARY goal, then delete the pair. | PARTIAL 2026-09-18 (still OPEN): `LAB_52aa` WoI gate (raw 88883-88885: during WoI score attacks only vs control-0 player slots or tribes) and the `FUN_465b_0000` best-defender rule in `ai_euro_score_move` / goal-dispatch drain loop ported literally; stand-in-free REF went from 2 surviving defended colonies to 1. Stand-in kept: DOS's odds core scores 0 vs any 2+-defender land stack (@UNIT col9 = 0), so assaults must come from the 0a60 MILITARY goal walk, whose binding the port loses when 0a60 housekeeping sets act_state 10 (raw 87566-87568) while the goal tail reads goals only at 0x0b. Leads: what DOS does at `+0x314c == 10`; uncited at-war early return in `ai_euro_move_scoring_gate`; normal-branch test narrower than DOS `local_5c < 0` (raw 88686). With stand-in, `golden_woi_ref01` passes, all colonies fall t21 (was t17). PARTIAL 2026-09-18c (still OPEN): `act_state` 10 fully decoded — `FUN_521d_0a60` raw 87567 is its sole writer, meaning "a foreign unit or settlement stands on one of my 8 neighbours", and DOS drops the unit's goal exactly as the port does (raw 88164 assigns goals only at 0/5/6), so the goal walk is NOT the assault route and lead (a)'s premise is refuted; 4d2e is. Ported the raw 90399-90404 tail demote; lead (b) at-war land-hunter early return deleted (20e6 has no war gate) and the drain loop's nation-blind `units_id_at` fallback fixed (own column on the step tile was handed to the attacker, freezing REF columns two tiles short) — with the stand-in all 7 colonies now fall t15 (was t21), without it 1 survives. Lead (c) refuted: raw 88686 is an inert ship-on-ocean skip gate; the port's `(here < 0 && pres < 0)` matches raw 88774-88776. Residue is DOS's own 0-odds core (`FUN_1427_0d38` case 0 = Σ `DS:0x5239`, byte-exact, 0 on land), so what makes the DOS REF break a 2+-defender colony needs a [live] DOSBox-X trace. Re-tested 2026-09-18e after the #522 fixes: the land arms now run without the stand-in (1368 `0x46` border-park exits) but 2 colonies survive, endgame 0 — the `LAB_52aa` odds core still scores 0 against any 2+-defender land stack, so `local_ce` is never set and DOS parks instead of assaulting. Stand-in kept; the [live] DOSBox-X trace is the sole remaining blocker. Static re-check 2026-09-22: the raw 88898 term is `((Σ0x5239[stack] + 1) / stack_count) * odds / max(1, 0x5239[attacker])`; @UNIT field 8 (→0x5239) is 0 for every infantry/cavalry row, 4 for Artillery, 12/20/90 for Privateer/Frigate/MoW, so an all-infantry 2+ stack still integer-divides to 0 while a stack holding Artillery does not; an Artillery attacker is divided by 4. Confirms the 0-odds residue; still [live]-blocked. FIXED 2026-09-22: no live trace was needed — the 0-odds core was a port bug: `ai_euro_20e6_unit_col9` returned the @UNIT guns column (0x523b: 0 for every land type) instead of DS:0x5239, which the @UNIT loader (raw 121115-121135: 0x5232 icon, 0x5234 moves×3, 0x5236 attack, 0x5235 defense, 0x5237 cargo, 0x5238 size, 0x5239 cost, 0x523a tools, 0x523b guns, 0x523c hull, 0x523d bits) fills from the **cost** column (Soldiers 2, Regulars 3, Artillery 6, …), so `((Σcost + 1) / stack) * base` is non-zero for stacked land defenders; the "field 8 → 0x5239" and "col9 → 0x5239" readings were both off by one column. Two more drain-side fixes fell out: an adjacent goto holding a foreign unit (the 4d2e attack pick, raw 88877 admits owner −1 via `local_10 < 4`; DOS then reads a stray relation byte at nation[n−1]+0x13b, stood in by the occupant's nation) is stepped into directly instead of being re-scored by `ai_euro_score_move`, which sidestepped diagonally around the foe. The stand-in pair and `ai_euro_colony_fort_bonus_at` are deleted; `golden_woi_ref01` without any stand-in: all 7 colonies fall t17, endgame latched; `golden_ai_turns` 6/6, ctest 68/68. Fixture trap: calloc'd layer3 = owner nibble 0 = nation 0, so hand-built maps must memset layer3 to 0xf0 before the attack term will score a foe (six test_ai_euro_war fixtures fixed). |

Port the ship-side goal producers, then restore the DOS binding. | FIXED 2026-09-19: premise was wrong — the DOS ocean-tile producers were already ported; the real blocker was `ai_euro_0a60_ship_full` counting passengers, while DOS raw 87511-87514 compares `0x5237[type]` with the goods-only `+0x3150` (the TURN2 save carries 2 colonists with it at 0), so loaded opening transports looked FOUND-eligible; made goods-only and the ship binding restored, `golden_ai_turns` 6/6, WoI endgame t16 -> t15, `unit_mow_war_transport_threatened` rewritten to the DOS CONTACT bind. |
| 528 | FIXED | AI turn-top act-state clear (`FUN_521d_0a60` raw 87560) is ported for values >= 10 only: DOS's 1/2/3 arm collides with the port's SENTRY landfall latch, which overloads the same `orders` value (`src/core/ai_euro.c`). Separate the landfall latch from `orders`, then port the full clear. | FIXED 2026-09-22: DOS's `orders == 1` (SENTRY) on a landed unit is simply the leftover "aboard ship" value `FUN_1427_10be` wrote at boarding (raw 8297/8674) — the LAB_3558 unload block never rewrites it, so a unit unloaded turn N still reads 1 at N's end, and this clear (N+1's own 0a60 top) wipes it to 0 before 20e6 re-decides, exactly matching the TURN2→3/TURN3→4 DOS save pairs. Ported the raw 87560-87564 `== 1 \|\| == 2 \|\| == 3` arm unconditionally (dropped the `ai_euro_ship_dos_enabled()` gate, `src/core/ai_euro.c` ~8204). Moved the port's own first-colony landfall goto memory off `orders == SENTRY` onto a new port-only `ColonizeUnit.ai_landfall_wait` bool (`src/core/units.h`, not saved — COLNXEXT does not carry it), set alongside `orders = UNITS_ORDER_SENTRY` at all 8 AI-unload/landfall sites in `ai_euro.c` (3 direct `ai_euro_set_goto` calls, 5 via `ai_euro_unload_pax_at`) and cleared by any non-SENTRY `ai_euro_set_goto` call. One reader moved to the flag (`ai_euro.c` ~19105, the war-land engage-adjacent gate); readers that only need real DOS order-1 semantics (`units_orders_skip_turn`, `ai_euro_land_is_passive_orders`) stayed on `orders`. Hit one real follow-on bug during verification: `units_wake` (`units.c`) decides whether to refund a unit's overnight-park MP by reading `prev == orders` at wake time, but by the time a landfall-waiting unit reaches its first-colony-land wake gate this turn, the new unconditional 0a60 clear has already zeroed `orders` to NONE, so the SENTRY-based park check no longer fired and TURN3→4 lost a unit's walk (stuck unmoved, MP fully but wrongly spent) — fixed by also treating `prev == NONE && ai_landfall_wait` as parked (still gated on the real `park_nights` counter so only an overnight park refunds, not same-turn spend). Since each golden TURN step reloads a fresh `.SAV` and the flag is not saved, col1 import (`col1_bridge.c`) now reconstructs it as `orders == SENTRY` at load time — a safe over-approximation matching this bug's own premise. `make test` 67/67, `golden_ai_turns` 6/6, `make golden` clean. |
| 529 | REFUTED | `ai_euro_land_is_fortified` excludes the `LAB_5a78` demote's `'0'`/5 idle marker — a measured divergence kept because the DOS-literal form cost a golden (`src/core/ai_euro.c`). Re-derive once #527/#528 land. | REFUTED 2026-09-22: the existing `orders ∈ {FORTIFY,FORTIFIED} && col1_ai_plan != 0x30` form is already DOS-literal, not a heuristic — the prior "DOS-literal" attempt was wrong to drop the ai_plan half instead of narrowing it. Two independent decomp facts, both re-verified this pass: (1) FUN_521d_20e6's shared exit tail (raw 90378-90386, `LAB_521d_589e`) forces `+0x314c`(`orders`) into the fortify family (5, or 6 when admitted) on *every* call exit, so `orders==FORTIFY/FORTIFIED` alone proves nothing — this is why the unconditional `return 1;` attempt broke `unit_garrison_quota_one_fortify`. (2) `LAB_5a78` (raw 90399-90404) writes `+0x314b='0'` **only** when `+0x314c` was already invalid (0 or 10) on entry — i.e. nothing upstream this call actually assigned the unit; every genuine assignment that reaches the same tail (own-colony garrison raw 87491-87492/88604-610, standing-military 'A' raw 87705 family, park 'F' raw 89025) sets `+0x314b` to a real code first. `!= '0'` is therefore exactly "a DOS branch decided this, not the idle default", matching the independent 'A'/'G' "already assigned" gates used elsewhere (`FUN_521d_0a60` re-decision skip, raw 88159, `+0x314b=='A'`; labor/combat-strength admission, raw 84724 and 87632, `!='G' && !='A'`) — none of those admit '0' either. Tried tightening to a pure `col1_ai_plan=='A'\|\|'G'` check (dropping the orders precondition, matching those three sites literally); it broke `unit_ai_euro_20e6`'s `unit_garrison_hold_blocks_scorer`, because `ai_euro_move_scoring_gate`'s own-colony "stay" branches stamp `col1_ai_plan='G'` without touching `orders` — that gap is real but belongs to `ai_euro_move_scoring_gate`/the LAB_5899 tail, not to this function, and is out of scope here. No code change; comment in `ai_euro_land_is_fortified` (`src/core/ai_euro.c` ~2009) rewritten with the full citation chain. `make test` 67/67, `make golden` clean, `AI_TURNS_ALL=1 golden_ai_turns` 6/6. |

| 851 | FIXED | Fur Trapper audit (M): factory-tier raw-material requirement is computed per-worker with round-half-up instead of DOS's colony-total floor divide. DOS `FUN_15eb_0bd4` raw 10168-10172: `local_6 = (gross_out << 1) / 3` — ONE floor divide over the colony's SUMMED finished-good gross, when `FUN_15eb_039e(chain) > 2` (factory tier). Port `colony_prod_manufacturing_input_row` (colony_production.c:195-201) returns `colony_prod_tier_input_for_output(FACTORY, out) = (out*6+8)/9` PER COLONIST and `colony_craft_pairs_next` (colony_craft.c:132) sums those. Scenario: one Fur Factory worker at output 10 (Master Fur Trader + Tory penalty) consumes 7 furs in the port, 6 in DOS; two workers at output 4 each consume 2+2=4 in the port, `(8*2)/3=5` in DOS. The 2026-08-15 player anchor (out 12 -> 8) is satisfied by both formulas, which is why it survived. Same divergence on rum/cigars/cloth/tools. New test_turn_core.c case factory_input_colony_total (1 free colonist sol 1: out 10 -> 6 furs; 2 criminals sol 1: out 4+4=8 -> 5 furs). (2) Commons secondary on Mountains flips to Silver Miner once its own Silver Deposit(12) is on the tile (the deposit's +2 effect overtakes Ore's bare-terrain lead), so "Hills/Mountains with a resource picks Ore over Silver" holds on Hills (whose own deposit, Ore Deposit, only widens Ore's lead) but not on Mountains. a618 tech-trim test skipped (not cheap under this pass's scope). |

| 897 | FIXED | Craft audit (H): factory-tier partial-input output is a proportional rescale, not DOS's unmet*3/2 back-conversion; Rum/Cigar/Cloth/Fur Factory output differs by 1-2 units when stored input is short. DOS: FUN_15eb_0bd4 raw 10159-10183 + 0b52 raw 10122-10139 + 0b0c raw 10102-10118: D=(G<<1)/3, U=D-(stock+gross_in) if >0, U'=(U*3)/2 (U'=G when U==D), Phase B out=G-U' (viceroy_unpacked.c:57238-57250). Port: colony_craft.c:273 `actual_out = total_out*actual_in/total_in`. G=9,D=6: S=1 DOS 2 vs port 1; S=3 5 vs 4; S=5 8 vs 7. Fix: factory tier `unmet=total_in-avail; out=total_out-((unmet==total_in)?total_out:(unmet*3)/2)` clamp 0; 1:1 tiers unchanged. | Confirmed against FUN_15eb_0bd4 (raw 10159-10183), FUN_15eb_0b96/0b52 and FUN_364b_0688 Phase B (raw 57238-57253). colony_craft.c's craft pass now computes `unmet = total_in - avail; if (unmet && total_in != total_out) unmet = (unmet == total_in) ? total_out : (unmet*3)/2; out = total_out - unmet` (clamped at 0) instead of the proportional `total_out*actual_in/total_in`; the 1:1 house/shop path is unchanged by construction (total_in == total_out makes the back-conversion a no-op). Input debit is still min(stock,total_in), which equals DOS's `stock += gross - demand` clamped at 0. Tests: tests/unit/test_colonies.c G=9 factory table S=0..6,9 -> 0,2,3,5,6,8,9,9 plus ore consumed, and a G=3 Blacksmith's House 1:1 table. |

| 911 | FIXED | Blacksmith/Gunsmith audit (M): Phase K "Need X." crumbs. DOS FUN_364b_0688 raw 57696-57728 has seven INDEPENDENT `if`s in GAME.TXT order (@LUMBER 0xe66, @COTTON, @TOBACCO, @CANESUGAR, @FURS, @ORE 0xe8b, @TOOLS 0xe8f), each `unmet[input] != 0 && net(output) == 0` on this tick's nets (0b50), not warehouse stock; the ore one is literally `unmet[ore] != 0 && 0b50(0xf) == 0b50(0xe)` (copy-paste quirk, port literally, DOS-LITERAL). DS scratch arrays: gross 0x8dc8, demand 0x8e0a, shortfall 0x8e32, unmet 0x8e5a, so 0x8e66 = unmet[ORE] (port comment at turn_production.c ~1536 says "paired tools+muskets", wrong). Port turn_production.c:1497-1543 is a single else-if chain in a different order with FOOD spliced in (one crumb max, ore outranks organics), gates tools on `stock[muskets]==0 && stock[tools]==0`, and has an invented "Need muskets." arm (no DS tag, no GAME.TXT section). Fix: seven independent ifs, DOS predicates, drop the muskets arm, keep #899 fix semantics. | Phase K now runs seven independent `if`s in DOS tag order (@LUMBER, @COTTON, @TOBACCO, @CANESUGAR, @FURS, @ORE, @TOOLS), each `unmet[input] != 0 && net(output) == 0` on this tick's values, with the ore arm's `net(muskets) == net(tools)` copy-paste quirk ported DOS-LITERAL and commented as deliberate (turn_production.c, FUN_364b_0688 raw 57696-57728; unmet base DS:0x8e5a stride 2, so 0x8e66 = unmet[ORE] and 0x8e76 = unmet[TOOLS] — the old "paired tools+muskets" comment is gone). `unmet[input] != 0` is read off a non-mutating colony_craft_preview over the pre-craft stock (`gross_out[out] < capacity_out[out]`, exactly colony_craft.c's `actual_out < total_out`); `net` is the colony's stock delta across the live craft pass, which is what #899's `gross_out == 0` reading approximated — right for rum/cigars/cloth/coats, but wrong for tools, whose net is docked by the gunsmith, i.e. the two cargos the @ORE/@TOOLS arms read. The invented "Need muskets." arm and the "Need food." arm spliced into the chain are deleted (DOS's food chrome is the VANISH/STARVE1/STARVE2/@FOODLOW block earlier in the function, kept where DOS has it).
